omplexity\"],[\"str\",\"Formulas\"]]\ttrue\tfalse\tfalse\ninstInhabitedDNF.default\t[[\"str\",\"instInhabitedDNF\"],[\"str\",\"default\"]]\t[[\"str\",\"TCSlib\"],[\"str\",\"Complexity\"],[\"str\",\"CircuitComplexity\"],[\"str\",\"Formulas\"]]\tfalse\tfalse\tfalse"
  let mut components : Std.HashMap String (String × String × String × String × String) := {}
  for line in (if componentPayload.isEmpty then [] else componentPayload.splitOn "\n") do
    match line.splitOn "\t" with
    | [n, nk, hk, i, m, a] =>
      if (components[n]?).isSome then throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: duplicate component request"
      components := components.insert n (nk, hk, i, m, a)
    | _ => throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: malformed component request"
  unless components.size == requests.size do throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: component count differs"
  let env ← Lean.getEnv
  let mut resolved : Array Lean.Name := #[]
  let mut catalog : Std.HashMap String (Array Lean.Name) := {}
  let mut verified : Nat := 0
  let mut active : Nat := 0
  let mut already : Nat := 0
  for (n, _) in env.constants.toList do
    let some mi := env.getModuleIdxFor? n | continue
    unless (`TCSlib).isPrefixOf env.header.moduleNames[mi.toNat]! do continue
    let ns := (catalog[n.toString]?).getD #[]
    catalog := catalog.insert n.toString (ns.push n)
  for (spelling, expectedHome) in requests do
    let ns := (catalog[spelling]?).getD #[]
    unless ns.size == 1 do throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: absent/ambiguous imported name {spelling}; candidates={ns.size}"
    let n := ns[0]!
    let some mi := env.getModuleIdxFor? n | throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: no home for {spelling}"
    let home := env.header.moduleNames[mi.toNat]!
    unless home.toString == expectedHome do throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: home differs for {spelling}: {home}"
    let some ci := env.find? n | throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: no constant for {spelling}"
    unless ci.isDefinition do throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: requested nondefinition {spelling}"
    let some (nk, hk, ei, em, ea) := components[spelling]?
      | throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: missing component request"
    unless nameKey n == nk && nameKey home == hk do
      throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: actual Name components differ"
    let instanceFlag := Lean.Meta.isInstanceCore env n
    let matcherFlag := (Lean.Meta.Match.Extension.getMatcherInfo? env n).isSome
    let abbrevFlag := match ci with
      | .defnInfo d => match d.hints with | .abbrev => true | _ => false
      | _ => false
    unless toString instanceFlag == ei && toString matcherFlag == em && toString abbrevFlag == ea do
      throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: definition/instance metadata differs"
    Lean.logInfo m!"G06-COMPONENT label={label} phase=install spelling={spelling} key={nk} home_key={hk} instance={instanceFlag}"
    let before ← Lean.Elab.Command.liftCoreM <| Lean.getReducibilityStatus n
    if before == .irreducible then
      already := already + 1
    else
      let id := Lean.mkIdent n
      Lean.Elab.Command.elabCommand (← `(command| attribute [local irreducible] $id:ident))
      active := active + 1
    let installed ← Lean.Elab.Command.liftCoreM <| Lean.getReducibilityStatus n
    unless installed == .irreducible do throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: seal not installed for {spelling}"
    resolved := resolved.push n
    verified := verified + 1
    Lean.logInfo m!"G06-NAME label={label} spelling={spelling} actual={n.toString} home={home} before={repr before} installed={repr installed}"
  unless active + already == requests.size do throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: installation count mismatch"
  unless verified == requests.size do throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: verification count mismatch"
  let maskExt := Lean.Meta.globalInstanceExtension
  let maskEnv ← Lean.getEnv
  let scopes := maskExt.ext.getState maskEnv
  unless scopes.stateStack.length == 1 do
    throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: unfolding mask requires base scope"
  let mut scopedNames : Std.HashSet Lean.Name := {}
  for (_, payloads) in scopes.scopedEntries.map.toList do
    for n in payloads do
      scopedNames := scopedNames.insert n
  let mut removed : Nat := 0
  for n in resolved do
    if scopedNames.contains n then
      throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: scoped unfolding payload intersects request {n}"
    if Lean.Meta.isGlobalInstance maskEnv n then
      removed := removed + 1
  -- InstanceEntry's BEq checks only val, so compare every field explicitly.
  let entryFields (e : Lean.Meta.InstanceEntry) :=
    (e.keys, Lean.ExprStructEq.mk e.val, e.priority, e.globalName?, e.synthOrder, e.attrKind)
  let lookupSnapshot (e : Lean.Environment) :=
    let s := Lean.Meta.instanceExtension.getState e
    (s.discrTree.toArray.map (fun (ks, entry) => (ks, entryFields entry)),
      s.instanceNames.toArray.map (fun (n, entry) => (n, entryFields entry)), s.erased.toList)
  let beforeLookup := lookupSnapshot env
  Lean.modifyEnv fun current =>
    maskExt.modifyState current fun membership =>
      resolved.foldl (fun s n => s.erase n) membership
  let maskedEnv ← Lean.getEnv
  unless beforeLookup == lookupSnapshot maskedEnv do
    throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: typeclass lookup state changed"
  for n in resolved do
    if Lean.Meta.isGlobalInstance maskedEnv n then
      throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: unfolding membership after mask for {n}"
  Lean.logInfo m!"G06-MASK label={label} phase=install requested={requests.size} verified={resolved.size} removed={removed} scope_depth=1 scoped_names={scopedNames.size} tc_state=preserved"
  Lean.logInfo m!"G06-SEAL label={label} requested={requests.size} active={active} already_irreducible={already} verified={verified}"


/-!
# Computational models: the catalog

Every model of computation in TCSlib, in one place. This facade imports each
model's defining file, so the catalog cannot silently rot: if a defining file
moves, this module stops compiling. `policy.md` §1 licenses a root-level
model *type* only when it is registered here; the model's operations and
lemmas still live in its own namespace.

Entries are alphabetical. Paths are relative to `TCSlib/`; machine files live
under `Complexity/`.

## Models

* `BoolCircuit.DAGCircuit n` — the book's Boolean circuits [AB09, Def 6.1]:
  topologically numbered DAGs of `∧`/`∨`/`¬` gates, size counting every vertex
  (`Complexity/CircuitComplexity/DAGCircuit.lean`).
* `BoolCircuit.DAGCircuitFamily` — non-uniform families of `DAGCircuit`s; the
  carrier of the book's `SIZE`, `P/poly`, `NC`, `AC`
  (`Complexity/CircuitComplexity/PPoly.lean`, `NCAC.lean`).
* `BoolCircuit.TreeCircuit n` — tree-shaped Boolean circuits (formulas):
  unbounded fan-in AND/OR nodes over polarity-carrying literals
  (`Complexity/CircuitComplexity/Basic.lean`).
* `BoolCircuit.LayeredCircuitFamily` — non-uniform families of `LayeredCircuit`
  circuits, one per input length; the carrier of `Language.InLayeredSIZE` and
  `P/poly` (`Complexity/CircuitComplexity/LayeredPPoly.lean`).
* `BoolCircuit.LayeredCircuit α inp out` — layered DAG circuits over an
  arbitrary alphabet; the raw model enforces no gate basis, and classes impose
  `stdGateOps` via `OnlyUsesGates`
  (`Complexity/CircuitComplexity/LayeredCircuit.lean`).
* `BoolCircuit.TreeCircuitFamily` — non-uniform families of tree circuits;
  the carrier of the formula classes `TreeNC` and `TreeAC`
  (`Complexity/CircuitComplexity/TreeNCAC.lean`).
* `BranchingProgram w n` — width-`w` branching programs on `n` inputs [Bar89, §1]: a list of
  instructions `⟨var, map0, map1⟩` with maps `Fin w → Fin w` (`map0` when the bit is `0`),
  yielding their composition in program order; `IsPermutation` when every map is a
  bijection (`Complexity/BranchingPrograms/Defs.lean`).
* `BranchingProgramFamily w` — non-uniform families of width-`w` branching programs with
  accepting sets `F_n` (`x ∈ L ↔ B(x) ∈ F_n`); the carrier of `Language.InBWBP` and
  `Language.InPBP` (`Complexity/BranchingPrograms/Defs.lean`).
* `CNF n` / `DNF n` — the two readings (AND of clauses / OR of terms) of a
  reading-neutral `Depth2 n` shape of `LitList n`s over `Literal n`; flat
  width-measured formulas for the switching-lemma development [OD14]
  (`Complexity/CircuitComplexity/Formulas.lean`).
* `Complexity.NUPPT` — non-uniform probabilistic polynomial-time machines
  [PS10, Definitions 23.4 and 26.8]: a `FinTM` clocked on every input, run on
  `⟨⟨⟨1^n, y⟩, advice n⟩, r⟩` with an explicit coin polynomial and advice
  polynomial; `Complexity.PPT` is the uniform subtype
  (`Complexity/Randomized/PPT.lean`).
* `DecisionTree n` — binary decision trees, with `dtDepth` the least depth
  computing a given function [OD14] (`BooleanAnalysis/DecisionTree.lean`).
* `FinNDTM` — bundled binary-choice nondeterministic machines; the carrier
  of `NTIME`, `NP`, `NEXP` (`Complexity/TuringMachine/Nondeterministic.lean`).
* `FinTM` — bundled deterministic machines; the carrier of `DTIME`, `P`,
  `EXP` (`Complexity/TuringMachine/Finite.lean`).
* `Light.Program` — programs of the light language [AFM26]: a list of procedure bodies over
  integer locals and a flat integer memory, with recursion, unit step costs and run limits;
  its program logic is `Light.Ends`/`Light.Meets`, and `Light.compileProgram` compiles it to the
  word RAM (`Programs/Light/`, vendored; design §15).
* `MultiTapeTM k Symbol State` — k-tape deterministic Turing machines with
  append-only output, the raw layer under `FinTM`
  (`Complexity/TuringMachine/Deterministic.lean`).
* `NDTM k Symbol State` — the raw layer under `FinNDTM`: binary-choice
  transitions, `List Bool` choice words
  (`Complexity/TuringMachine/Nondeterministic.lean`).
* `NPReductions.CNFFormula V` — the legacy variable-indexed CNF used by the
  SAT→3SAT reduction and the Tseitin encoding
  (`Complexity/NPReductions/SATTo3SAT.lean`).
* `OracleTM k Symbol State` — oracle machines with a dedicated query tape
  and one-step answers (`Complexity/TuringMachine/Oracle.lean`).
* `Std.Sat.CNF ℕ` — Lean core's clause-list CNF, the campaign's SAT/3SAT
  carrier; TCSlib's layer over it is in `Complexity/Formulas/CNF.lean`,
  with serialization in `Complexity/Formulas/CNFEncoding.lean`.
* `Std.Sat.DNF` — the same list-of-literal-lists shape read as an OR of ANDs,
  in its own type; the TAUTOLOGY carrier (`Complexity/Formulas/DNF.lean`).
* `WordRAM.Instr` (programs `List WordRAM.Instr`) — the word RAM of [AFM26]: W-bit words, nine
  instructions (constant 1, add, subtract, multiply, indirect load and store, branch on negative,
  accept, reject), runs by `WordRAM.exec` (`Programs/WordRAM/`, vendored; design §15).
* `WordRAM.XInstr` (programs `WordRAM.XProgram`) — the word RAM extended by a wrapper: the nine
  instructions as `XInstr.base`, plus input read in place, output, read-once coins and halving;
  runs by `WordRAM.xstep`, with footprints (`Programs/WordRAM/Extended.lean`, not vendored;
  design §15d).

## Conversions

Model-to-model maps (pointers only — their files are not imported here):

* `WordRAM.exists_simulation` / `WordRAM.exists_ndSimulation` — extended word-RAM programs →
  `Turing.FinTM` / `Turing.FinNDTM`, with explicit time and space bounds (stage 2;
  `Complexity/RAMSimulation/Simulation.lean`; statements gated, proofs pending).

* `BoolCircuit.TreeCircuit.toDAG` / `BoolCircuit.DAGCircuit.toTree` — formulas → DAGs
  (linear) and DAGs → formulas (exponential in depth)
  (`Complexity/CircuitComplexity/TreeDAG.lean`).
* `BoolCircuit.DAGCircuit.toLayered` / `BoolCircuit.LayeredCircuit.toDAG` — DAGs ↔ layered
  circuits over `stdGateOps`, polynomial both ways; `BoolCircuit.TreeCircuit.toLayered` —
  formulas → layered circuits, gate by gate
  (`Complexity/CircuitComplexity/LayeredDAG.lean`).
* `BoolCircuit.DAGCircuit.binarize` / `BoolCircuit.DAGCircuit.deMorgan` — fan-in reduction
  and `∨`-elimination on DAGs (`Complexity/CircuitComplexity/DAGFanin.lean`).
* `BoolCircuit.DAGCircuit.layerNAND` — NAND-form DAGs → layered NAND-form DAGs on the `2n`
  literal inputs, depth preserved exactly (`Complexity/CircuitComplexity/NANDLayering.lean`).
* `BoolCircuit.DAGCircuit.toNANDForm` — fan-in-≤ 2 DAGs → NAND form (`¬` gates of fan-in `≤ 2`),
  depth at most doubled; an instance of `DAGCircuit.rewrite`
  (`Complexity/CircuitComplexity/NANDForm.lean`).
* `BoolCircuit.TreeCircuit.toLayeredWrapper` — a semantic wrapper, not an embedding:
  the tree's evaluation becomes a single unrestricted first-layer gate, so only
  evaluation is preserved, with no general gate-basis guarantee
  (`Complexity/CircuitComplexity/LayeredCircuit.lean`).
* `BoolCircuit.TreeCircuit.tseitin` / `TreeCircuit.toCNF` — circuits →
  equisatisfiable `NPReductions.CNFFormula`
  (`Complexity/CircuitComplexity/CircuitSat.lean`).
* `BoolCircuit.LayeredCircuit.toTreeCircuit` — DAG → tree unrolling, exponential
  in depth (`Complexity/CircuitComplexity/LayeredCircuit.lean`).
* `BranchingProgram.barrington` — fan-in-2 formulas (`BoolCircuit.TreeCircuit`) → width-5
  permutation branching programs of length `≤ 4^depth`, computable [Bar89, Thm 1]
  (`Complexity/BranchingPrograms/Barrington.lean`).
* `BranchingProgram.toFormula` — width-`w` branching programs with an accepting set → fan-in-2
  formulas of depth `O(log length)` [Bar89, Thm 2]
  (`Complexity/BranchingPrograms/BarringtonConverse.lean`).
* `FinTM.toFinNDTM` — deterministic machines as nondeterministic ones, in
  lockstep (`Complexity/TuringMachine/Nondeterministic.lean`).
* `LMN.NormalFormConversion` — normal-form tree circuits ↔ `CNF`/`DNF`
  formulas (`BooleanAnalysis/LMN/NormalFormConversion.lean`).
* `MultiTapeTM.toNDTM` — the raw-layer deterministic → nondeterministic
  embedding (`Complexity/TuringMachine/Nondeterministic.lean`).
* `OracleTM.ofMultiTapeTM` — plain machines as oracle machines that never
  query (`Complexity/TuringMachine/Oracle.lean`).

## References

* [AB09] S. Arora, B. Barak, *Computational Complexity: A Modern Approach*,
  Cambridge University Press, 2009.
* [Bar89] D. A. Barrington, *Bounded-width polynomial-size branching programs recognize exactly
  those languages in NC¹*, J. Comput. Syst. Sci. 38(1):150–164, 1989.
* [OD14] R. O'Donnell, *Analysis of Boolean Functions*, Cambridge University
  Press, 2014.
* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed., January 2010.
* [AFM26] anthropics/formal-math, folder `3sum-apsp`, commit e1a4e650, Apache-2.0 (the light language and the word RAM, vendored under `Programs/`).

/- G06-HARNESS EOF-VERIFY SealA_ComputationalModels -/
set_option maxHeartbeats 0
run_cmd do

omplexity\"],[\"str\",\"Formulas\"]]\ttrue\tfalse\tfalse\ninstInhabitedDNF.default\t[[\"str\",\"instInhabitedDNF\"],[\"str\",\"default\"]]\t[[\"str\",\"TCSlib\"],[\"str\",\"Complexity\"],[\"str\",\"CircuitComplexity\"],[\"str\",\"Formulas\"]]\tfalse\tfalse\tfalse"
  let mut components : Std.HashMap String (String × String × String × String × String) := {}
  for line in (if componentPayload.isEmpty then [] else componentPayload.splitOn "\n") do
    match line.splitOn "\t" with
    | [n, nk, hk, i, m, a] =>
      if (components[n]?).isSome then throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: duplicate component request"
      components := components.insert n (nk, hk, i, m, a)
    | _ => throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: malformed component request"
  unless components.size == requests.size do throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: component count differs"
  let env ← Lean.getEnv
  let mut catalog : Std.HashMap String (Array Lean.Name) := {}
  let mut verified : Nat := 0
  for (n, _) in env.constants.toList do
    let some mi := env.getModuleIdxFor? n | continue
    unless (`TCSlib).isPrefixOf env.header.moduleNames[mi.toNat]! do continue
    let ns := (catalog[n.toString]?).getD #[]
    catalog := catalog.insert n.toString (ns.push n)
  for (spelling, expectedHome) in requests do
    let ns := (catalog[spelling]?).getD #[]
    unless ns.size == 1 do throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: absent/ambiguous imported name {spelling}; candidates={ns.size}"
    let n := ns[0]!
    let some mi := env.getModuleIdxFor? n | throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: no home for {spelling}"
    let home := env.header.moduleNames[mi.toNat]!
    unless home.toString == expectedHome do throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: home differs for {spelling}: {home}"
    let some ci := env.find? n | throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: no constant for {spelling}"
    unless ci.isDefinition do throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: requested nondefinition {spelling}"
    let some (nk, hk, ei, em, ea) := components[spelling]?
      | throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: missing component request"
    unless nameKey n == nk && nameKey home == hk do
      throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: actual Name components differ"
    let instanceFlag := Lean.Meta.isInstanceCore env n
    let matcherFlag := (Lean.Meta.Match.Extension.getMatcherInfo? env n).isSome
    let abbrevFlag := match ci with
      | .defnInfo d => match d.hints with | .abbrev => true | _ => false
      | _ => false
    unless toString instanceFlag == ei && toString matcherFlag == em && toString abbrevFlag == ea do
      throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: definition/instance metadata differs"
    Lean.logInfo m!"G06-COMPONENT label={label} phase=eof spelling={spelling} key={nk} home_key={hk} instance={instanceFlag}"
    let installed ← Lean.Elab.Command.liftCoreM <| Lean.getReducibilityStatus n
    unless installed == .irreducible do throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: seal not installed at EOF for {spelling}"
    if Lean.Meta.isGlobalInstance env n then
      throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: unfolding membership at EOF for {spelling}"
    verified := verified + 1
  unless verified == requests.size do throwError "G06-HARNESS {label}: verification count mismatch"
  Lean.logInfo m!"G06-MASK label={label} phase=eof requested={requests.size} verified={verified}"
  Lean.logInfo m!"G06-SEAL-END label={label} requested={requests.size} verified={verified}"
